  323: 
  324: def add_observation(
  325:     table: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
  326:     entry: dict[str, Any],
  327:     target_metrics: dict[str, Any],
  328:     mode: str,
  329:     capture: str,
  330:     join_status: str,
  331:     scope_mismatch_metrics: set[str] | None = None,
  332: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  333:     name = entry["metric"]
  334:     workload_family = capture.split("_", 1)[0]
  335:     row = table.setdefault(name, {
  336:         # `family` is retained as a compatibility alias for the metric family.
  337:         "family": family(name),
  338:         "metric_family": family(name),
  339:         "workload_families": set(),
  340:         "modes": set(),
  341:         "prediction_status": collections.Counter(),
  342:         "reasons": collections.Counter(),
  343:         "predicted": 0,
  344:         "compared": 0,
  345:         "target_available": 0,
  346:         "scope_mismatch": 0,
  347:         "unit_mismatch": 0,
  348:         "target_missing": 0,
  349:         "zero_opportunity": 0,
  350:         "prediction_value_missing": 0,
  351:         "predicted_zero_on_zero_target": 0,
  352:         "predicted_nonzero_on_zero_target": 0,
  353:         "errors": [],
  354:         "relative_errors": [],
  355:         "errors_by_capture": collections.defaultdict(list),
  356:         "relative_by_family": collections.defaultdict(list),
  357:         "relative_by_workload_family": collections.defaultdict(list),
  358:         "relative_by_join": collections.defaultdict(list),
  359:         "source_counts": collections.Counter(),
  360:         "aliases_not_scored": 0,
  361:         "different_numeric_alternatives": 0,
  362:         "alternative_conflicts": 0,
  363:         "zero_targets": 0,
  364:         "exact": 0,
  365:         "captures": set(),
  366:     })
  367:     row["modes"].add(mode)
  368:     row["captures"].add(capture)
  369:     row["workload_families"].add(workload_family)
  370:     status = entry["status"]
  371:     row["prediction_status"][status] += 1
  372:     if entry.get("reason"):
  373:         row["reasons"][entry["reason"]] += 1
  374:     estimate = entry.get("estimate")
  375:     row["source_counts"][entry["source_key"]] += 1
  376:     alternatives = entry.get("alternative_predictions", [])
  377:     row["aliases_not_scored"] += len(alternatives)
  378:     row["different_numeric_alternatives"] += sum(
  379:         candidate["status"] == "predicted"
  380:         and candidate.get("estimate") is not None
  381:         and estimate is not None
  382:         and candidate["estimate"] != estimate
  383:         for candidate in alternatives
  384:     )
  385:     if entry.get("alternative_conflict"):
  386:         row["alternative_conflicts"] += 1
  387:     result = {"metric": name, "source_key": entry["source_key"],
  388:               "estimate": estimate, "model_status": status, "model_unit": entry["unit"]}
  389:     if entry.get("semantics"):
  390:         result["semantics"] = entry["semantics"]
  391:     if alternatives:
  392:         result["alternative_predictions"] = alternatives
  393:     if entry.get("alternative_conflict"):
  394:         result["alternative_conflict"] = True
  395:     # A shape/order fallback is useful coverage metadata but is not an exact
  396:     # launch identity. Never score it as NCU accuracy: the existing SM86
  397:     # profiles contain one selected kernel row while many traces contain many
  398:     # launches, and profiler-off names are often ambiguous.
  399:     if join_status != "identity":
  400:         return {**result, "status": "join_not_exact", "join_status": join_status}
  401:     target = target_metrics.get(name, {})
  402:     truth = finite(target.get("value")) if isinstance(target, dict) else None
  403:     if status != "predicted" or estimate is None:
  404:         if status == "predicted" and estimate is None:
  405:             row["prediction_value_missing"] += 1
  406:         return {**result, "status": "model_unavailable", "reason": entry.get("reason")}
  407:     row["predicted"] += 1
  408:     if truth is None:
  409:         row["target_missing"] += 1
  410:         return {**result, "status": "target_missing"}
  411:     row["target_available"] += 1
  412:     result.update(target=truth, target_unit=target.get("unit"))
  413:     if scope_mismatch_metrics and name in scope_mismatch_metrics:
  414:         row["scope_mismatch"] += 1
  415:         result["scope_mismatch_reason"] = "independent NCU/trace operation scope warning"
  416:         return {**result, "status": "scope_mismatch"}
  417:     if truth == 0:
  418:         row["zero_opportunity"] += 1
  419:         if estimate == 0:
  420:             row["predicted_zero_on_zero_target"] += 1
  421:         else:
  422:             row["predicted_nonzero_on_zero_target"] += 1
  423:     # NCU exports these documented count metrics with an empty unit on SM120.
  424:     # This is a name-qualified count relation, not permission to equate arbitrary
  425:     # unitless metrics with wavefronts or to convert timing domains.
  426:     shared_wavefront = name in {
  427:         "l1tex__data_pipe_lsu_wavefronts_mem_shared_op_ld.sum",
  428:         "l1tex__data_pipe_lsu_wavefronts_mem_shared_op_st.sum",
  429:         "l1tex__data_pipe_lsu_wavefronts_mem_shared_op_atom.sum",
  430:         "l1tex__data_bank_conflicts_pipe_lsu_mem_shared_op_ld.sum",
  431:         "l1tex__data_bank_conflicts_pipe_lsu_mem_shared_op_st.sum",
  432:         "l1tex__data_bank_conflicts_pipe_lsu_mem_shared_op_atom.sum",
  433:     } and entry["unit"] == "wavefront" and target.get("unit") == ""
  434:     if target.get("unit") not in allowed_units(entry["unit"]) and not shared_wavefront:
  435:         row["unit_mismatch"] += 1
  436:         return {**result, "status": "unit_mismatch"}
  437:     error = abs(estimate - truth)
  438:     row["compared"] += 1
  439:     row["errors"].append(error)
  440:     row["errors_by_capture"][capture].append(error)
===== END tools/sm120_metric_report.py: scope diagnostics =====


===== BEGIN tools/sm120_metric_report.py: reducer summary/report =====
  568: def main() -> int:
  569:     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
  570:     parser.add_argument("--full", required=True, type=Path)
  571:     parser.add_argument("--fast", required=True, type=Path)
  572:     parser.add_argument("--profiles", required=True, type=Path)
  573:     parser.add_argument("--output", required=True, type=Path)
  574:     args = parser.parse_args()
  575:     args.output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  576:     tables: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {"full": {}, "fast": {}}
  577:     target_inventory: collections.Counter = collections.Counter()
  578:     with open_jsonl_writer(args.output / "metric_comparisons.jsonl") as output:
  579:         full_counts = reduce_mode(args.full, "full", args.profiles, output, tables["full"], target_inventory)
  580:         fast_counts = reduce_mode(args.fast, "fast", args.profiles, output, tables["fast"], target_inventory)
  581:     full_counts["scope_mismatch_predictions"] = sum(
  582:         row["scope_mismatch"] for row in tables["full"].values()
  583:     )
  584:     fast_counts["scope_mismatch_predictions"] = sum(
  585:         row["scope_mismatch"] for row in tables["fast"].values()
  586:     )
  587:     by_mode = {mode: render_summary(table) for mode, table in tables.items()}
  588:     available = {name for table in tables.values() for name, row in table.items() if row["predicted"]}
  589:     declared = {name for table in tables.values() for name in table}
  590:     unavailable: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = collections.defaultdict(list)
  591:     for name in sorted((set(target_inventory) | declared) - available):
  592:         unavailable[family(name)].append({"metric": name, "target_rows": target_inventory[name]})
  593:     summary = {
  594:         "schema_version": 4,
  595:         "scope": "post-replay metric predictions; NCU values never replay/model inputs",
  596:         "comparison_view": "scope_compatible_secondary",
  597:         "raw_all_row_ape_authoritative": True,
  598:         "raw_all_row_ape_included": False,
  599:         "scope_mismatch_policy": "retain_rows_exclude_from_raw_all_row_comparison_ape",
  600:         "selection": "one prediction per metric/launch/mode; finite declared metric, cache observer, native metric, then timing fallback; alternatives retained but not scored; no NCU-based selection",
  601:         "sources": {"full": str(args.full), "fast": str(args.fast), "profiles": str(args.profiles)},
  602:         "jsonl_compression": jsonl_compression_description(),
  603:         "full": full_counts,
  604:         "fast": fast_counts,
  605:         "by_mode": by_mode,
  606:         "available_metric_count": len(available),
  607:         "unavailable_metric_count": sum(map(len, unavailable.values())),
  608:         "unavailable_by_family": dict(sorted(unavailable.items())),
  609:         "conflicted_metric_count": sum(
  610:             mode_summary["conflicted_metric_count"] for mode_summary in by_mode.values()
  611:         ),
  612:     }
  613:     (args.output / "summary.json").write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2) + "\n")
  614:     with (args.output / "summary.md").open("w") as report:
  615:         report.write("# SM86 metric coverage\n\n")
  616:         report.write("Post-replay reduction of emitted predictions; NCU values were not replay inputs.\n\n")
  617:         report.write("Each exact launch contributes at most once per metric and mode. Full and fast errors are not pooled; aliases and differing alternatives are retained in compressed rows, not counted as extra observations. Ordered/shape fallback joins remain coverage metadata and are excluded from raw/all-row comparison APE. Relative errors exclude zero targets.\n\n")
  618:         for mode, mode_summary in by_mode.items():
  619:             report.write(f"## {mode.capitalize()} scope-compatible compared metrics\n\n| metric | family | compared | median absolute error | P90 | mean APE | median APE | P90 APE | exact |\n|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|\n")
  620:             for name, item in mode_summary["metrics"].items():
  621:                 if item["compared"] == 0:
  622:                     continue
  623:                 error = item["absolute_error"]
  624:                 relative = item["relative_error_nonzero_targets"]
  625:                 mean_ape = relative.get("mean")
  626:                 report.write(f"| `{name}` | {item['family']} | {item['compared']} | {error['median']} | {error['p90']} | {mean_ape} | {relative['median']} | {relative['p90']} | {item['exact']} |\n")
  627:         report.write("\n## Currently unavailable model families\n\n")
  628:         report.write("Metrics with no simulator prediction in either retained mode are listed once by family; raw NCU distributions are intentionally omitted.\n\n")
  629:         for family_name, entries in summary["unavailable_by_family"].items():
  630:             names = ", ".join(
  631:                 f"`{entry['metric']}` ({entry['target_rows']} target rows)" for entry in entries
  632:             )
  633:             report.write(f"- **{family_name}:** {names}\n")
  634:         report.write("\n## Post-replay scope diagnostics\n\n")
  635:         report.write("Opcode-scope warnings flag gross disagreement between trace-side logical memory counts and an independent NCU opcode metric. They never alter replay or prediction selection. Explicit scope-mismatch rows remain in compressed `metric_comparisons.jsonl.zst` and are excluded from raw/all-row comparison APE; the resulting scope-compatible table is a secondary like-for-like view.\n\n")
  636:         report.write(f"- Full warnings: **{summary['full'].get('scope_warnings', 0)}**; fast warnings: **{summary['fast'].get('scope_warnings', 0)}**.\n")
  637:         report.write(f"- Full predictions excluded from raw/all-row comparison APE for explicit scope mismatch: **{summary['full'].get('scope_mismatch_predictions', 0)}**; fast: **{summary['fast'].get('scope_mismatch_predictions', 0)}**.\n")
  638:         report.write("\n## Conflicting prediction alternatives\n\n")
  639:         report.write("Metrics with incompatible finite alternatives are retained in compressed rows but excluded from scoring until endpoint/scope provenance selects one.\n\n")
  640:         for mode, mode_summary in by_mode.items():

 1988: fn tail_report(tails: TailFlags) -> TailReport {
 1989:     TailReport {
 1990:         reduction_read_modify_write: tails.red_rmw,
 1991:         dirty_writeback: tails.dirty_writeback,
 1992:         coherence: tails.coherence,
 1993:         async_copy_shared_visibility: tails.ldgsts_dst_shared_timing,
 1994:     }
 1995: }
 1996: 
 1997: pub(crate) fn sm120_front_end_envelope_cycles(
 1998:     result: &FullKernelReplayResult,
 1999: ) -> Option<u64> {
 2000:     if result.arch() != sim_arch::ArchId::Sm120 {
 2001:         return None;
 2002:     }
 2003:     let config = Sm120AlphaConfig::from_m8_m9();
 2004:     if config.front_end_line_cycles_denominator == 0 {
 2005:         return None;
 2006:     }
 2007:     let lines = result.front_end().compulsory_instruction_read_sectors();
 2008:     let scaled = lines
 2009:         .checked_mul(config.front_end_line_cycles_numerator)?
 2010:         .checked_add(config.front_end_line_cycles_denominator - 1)?
 2011:         / config.front_end_line_cycles_denominator;
 2012:     config.front_end_base_cycles.checked_add(scaled)
 2013: }
 2014: 
 2015: fn effective_gpc_completion_frontier_report(
 2016:     result: &FullKernelReplayResult,
 2017: ) -> Result<CompletionFrontierReport, RunError> {
 2018:     let envelope = sm120_front_end_envelope_cycles(result).unwrap_or(0);
 2019:     let add = |cycles: Option<u64>| match cycles {
 2020:         Some(value) => value
 2021:             .checked_add(envelope)
 2022:             .map(Some)
 2023:             .ok_or_else(|| RunError::new("timing projection", "GPC front-end envelope overflow")),
 2024:         None => Ok(None),
 2025:     };
 2026:     Ok(match result.modeled_completion_frontier() {
 2027:         ModeledCompletionFrontier::Complete { cycles } => {
 2028:             CompletionFrontierReport::Complete { cycles: add(cycles)? }
 2029:         }
 2030:         ModeledCompletionFrontier::Incomplete {
 2031:             lower_bound_cycles,
 2032:             unmodeled_tails,
 2033:         } => CompletionFrontierReport::IncompleteUnmodeledRetirementTails {
 2034:             lower_bound_cycles: add(lower_bound_cycles)?,
 2035:             unmodeled_tails: tail_report(unmodeled_tails),
 2036:         },
 2037:     })
 2038: }

 2089:             writeback_completed_bytes: value.writeback_completed_bytes,

===== BEGIN sim/direct_run/src/lib.rs: DRAM construction =====
 2600:     }
 2601:     if config.sm120_effective_l1.is_some()
 2602:         && (!config.sm120_alpha || config.memory_mode == MemoryMode::Flat)
 2603:     {
 2604:         return Err(RunError::new(
 2605:             "arguments",
 2606:             "--sm120-effective-l1 requires --sm120-alpha with --mode l1 or --mode l1-l2",
 2607:         ));
 2608:     }
 2609:     if config.sm120_effective_l2.is_some()
 2610:         && (!config.sm120_alpha || config.memory_mode != MemoryMode::L1L2)
 2611:     {
 2612:         return Err(RunError::new(
 2613:             "arguments",
 2614:             "--sm120-effective-l2 requires --sm120-alpha with --mode l1-l2",
 2615:         ));
 2616:     }
 2617:     if config.dirty_writeback_capacity_sectors == Some(0) {
 2618:         return Err(RunError::new(
 2619:             "arguments",
 2620:             "dirty writeback capacity must be positive",
 2621:         ));
 2622:     }
 2623:     if config.dirty_writeback_capacity_sectors.is_some()
 2624:         && (config.memory_mode != MemoryMode::L1L2 || !config.carded_dram_bandwidth)
 2625:     {
 2626:         return Err(RunError::new(
 2627:             "arguments",
 2628:             "dirty writeback requires --mode l1-l2 together with --carded-dram",
 2629:         ));
 2630:     }
 2631:     if config.coherence_service_cycles == Some(0) || config.coherence_capacity_sectors == Some(0) {
 2632:         return Err(RunError::new(
 2633:             "arguments",
 2634:             "coherence service cycles and capacity must be positive",
 2635:         ));
 2636:     }
 2637:     if config.coherence_capacity_sectors.is_some() != config.coherence_service_cycles.is_some() {
 2638:         return Err(RunError::new(
 2639:             "arguments",
 2640:             "coherence service cycles and capacity must be supplied together",
 2641:         ));
 2642:     }
 2643:     if config.l1_visibility_protocol && config.coherence_service_cycles.is_none() {
 2644:         return Err(RunError::new(
 2645:             "arguments",
 2646:             "L1 visibility protocol requires --coherence-service-cycles and --coherence-capacity-sectors",
 2647:         ));
 2648:     }
 2649:     if config.l1_resident_writeback
 2650:         && (config.coherence_service_cycles.is_none()
 2651:             || config.dirty_writeback_capacity_sectors.is_none()
 2652:             || config.memory_mode != MemoryMode::L1L2
 2653:             || !config.carded_dram_bandwidth
 2654:             || !config.l1_visibility_protocol)
 2655:     {
 2656:         return Err(RunError::new(
 2657:             "arguments",
 2658:             "L1 resident writeback requires l1-l2, --carded-dram, --dirty-writeback-capacity-sectors, the coherence service pair, and --l1-visibility-protocol",
 2659:         ));
 2660:     }
 2661:     if config.coherence_service_cycles.is_some() && config.memory_mode != MemoryMode::L1L2 {
 2662:         return Err(RunError::new(
 2663:             "arguments",
 2664:             "coherence service requires --mode l1-l2",
 2665:         ));
 2666:     }
 2667:     if config.memory_mode == MemoryMode::Flat {
 2668:         if config.carded_dram_bandwidth {
 2669:             return Err(RunError::new(
 2670:                 "arguments",
 2671:                 "--carded-dram requires --mode l1-l2",
 2672:             ));
 2673:         }
 2674:         return Ok(ReplayMemMode::Flat);
 2675:     }
 2676: 
 2677:     let mut l1 = if let Some(effective) = config.sm120_effective_l1 {
 2678:         effective.as_l1_config()
 2679:     } else {
 2680:         let pchase_path = config.pchase_card.as_ref().ok_or_else(|| {
 2681:             RunError::new(
 2682:                 "arguments",
 2683:                 "--pchase-card is required for l1 and l1-l2 modes",
 2684:             )
 2685:         })?;
 2686:         let pchase = load_pchase_card(pchase_path)?;
 2687:         let geometry = pchase
 2688:             .resolve_walk_geometry_for_arch(issue.arch(), None)
 2689:             .map_err(at("L1 geometry"))?;
 2690:         issue.l1_config(geometry.capacity.line_capacity, geometry.mshr.count)
 2691:     };
 2692:     if issue.arch() == sim_arch::ArchId::Sm86 && config.sm120_effective_l1.is_none() {
 2693:         let geometry = arch_card
 2694:             .require_l1_geometry()
 2695:             .map_err(at("SM86 L1 set geometry"))?;
 2696:         // The card documents the set/way domain, while the p-chase resolver
 2697:         // retains the active carveout capacity.  Keep both sources explicit:
 2698:         // the pressure-bounded modulo/folded candidate is an effective
 2699:         // scenario and the proprietary hash remains unknown; global capacity
 2700:         // still follows the p-chase point.
 2701:         l1.set_count = Some(geometry.sets);
 2702:         l1.ways = Some(geometry.ways);
 2703:         l1.set_index = L1SetIndex::ModuloXorFallback;
 2704:     }
 2705:     if config.memory_mode == MemoryMode::L1 {
 2706:         if config.carded_dram_bandwidth {
 2707:             return Err(RunError::new(
 2708:                 "arguments",
 2709:                 "--carded-dram requires --mode l1-l2",
 2710:             ));
 2711:         }
 2712:         return Ok(ReplayMemMode::L1Const(l1));
 2713:     }
 2714: 
 2715:     let l2 = if let Some(effective) = config.sm120_effective_l2 {
 2716:         if effective.write_policy != L2_WRITE_ALLOCATE_POLICY {
 2717:             return Err(RunError::new(
 2718:                 "SM120 effective L2 configuration",
 2719:                 format!(
 2720:                     "unsupported effective L2 write policy {:?}; expected {:?}",
 2721:                     effective.write_policy, L2_WRITE_ALLOCATE_POLICY
 2722:                 ),
 2723:             ));
 2724:         }
 2725:         L2Config::from_effective_geometry_for_arch(
 2726:             issue.arch(),
 2727:             effective.capacity_bytes,
 2728:             effective.line_bytes,
 2729:             effective.sector_bytes,
 2730:             effective.num_slices,
 2731:             effective.associativity,
 2732:             effective.replacement,
 2733:             effective.slice_hash,
 2734:         )
 2735:         .map_err(at("SM120 effective L2 configuration"))?
 2736:     } else {
 2737:         let l2_geometry = arch_card.require_l2_geometry().map_err(at("L2 geometry"))?;
 2738:         L2Config::from_carded_geometry_for_arch(
 2739:             issue.arch(),
 2740:             l2_geometry.capacity_bytes,
 2741:             l2_geometry.line_bytes,
 2742:             l2_geometry.sector_bytes,
 2743:             l2_geometry.num_slices,
 2744:             l2_geometry.policy.as_deref(),
 2745:         )
 2746:         .map_err(at("L2 configuration"))?
 2747:     };
 2748:     let dram = if config.carded_dram_bandwidth {
 2749:         let card = arch_card
 2750:             .require_dram_bandwidth()
 2751:             .map_err(at("DRAM bandwidth"))?;
 2752:         let bytes_per_sec = config
 2753:             .dram_service_rate_bytes_per_sec
 2754:             .or_else(|| card.validated_peak_bytes_per_second().ok())
 2755:             .ok_or_else(|| RunError::new("DRAM bandwidth", "no validated service rate"))?;
 2756:         if bytes_per_sec == 0 {
 2757:             return Err(RunError::new("DRAM bandwidth", "service rate must be positive"));
 2758:         }
 2759:         let _card_peak = card
 2760:             .validated_peak_bytes_per_second()
 2761:             .map_err(at("DRAM bandwidth"))?;
 2762:         let sm_clock_hz = card.locked_sm_clock_hz().map_err(at("DRAM clock"))?;
 2763:         match config.dirty_writeback_capacity_sectors {
 2764:             Some(capacity_sectors) => DramBw::CardedWriteback {
 2765:                 arch: issue.arch(),
 2766:                 bytes_per_sec,
 2767:                 sm_clock_hz,
 2768:                 capacity_sectors,
 2769:             },
 2770:             None => DramBw::Carded {
 2771:                 arch: issue.arch(),
 2772:                 bytes_per_sec,
 2773:                 sm_clock_hz,
 2774:             },
 2775:         }
 2776:     } else {
 2777:         DramBw::Unmodeled { arch: issue.arch() }
 2778:     };
 2779:     let coherence = config
 2780:         .coherence_service_cycles
===== END sim/direct_run/src/lib.rs: DRAM construction =====

 2600:     origin_source_hits: [u64; 2],

 2633:     /// a lookup miss that reuses an older allocation's ready frontier; a new
 2634:     /// fill owns a new lower-level fetch capability. These values never alter

 2641: 
 2642: /// Aggregate DRAM read-bandwidth model (the §6.3 "DRAM
 2643: /// bandwidth-latency" increment). The configured carded rate is a model
 2644: /// covariate: the corpus runner defaults to the independently measured
 2645: /// 825 GB/s effective-rate hypothesis, while direct `sim-run` without an
 2646: /// override falls back to the architecture-card peak. Neither value is a
 2647: /// physical controller claim; the model is one G/D/1 byte server over the shared hierarchy
 2648: /// read-miss sector stream: each fetching sector demands 32 B, and a
 2649: /// reply-carrying request or intrinsically returning ATOM cannot complete
 2650: /// before BOTH its latency arm and the server's completion of ITS OWN request's
 2651: /// bytes. Late L1 merges carry their own sector response, so the waiting warp
 2652: /// clears at that child sector's ready event. Uncongested streams see pure
 2653: /// latency (the server only ever ADDS delay).
 2654: ///
 2655: /// Default `Carded` scope remains DRAM READS only — write data validates Store
 2656: /// sectors without a fetch, while returning ATOM and non-returning RED charge
 2657: /// missing sectors for their internal RMW read. The opt-in `CardedWriteback`
 2658: /// variant adds a bounded dirty-sector service lane; coherence ownership and
 2659: /// visibility, and RED's value-dependent serialization, remain typed tails.
 2660: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
 2661: pub enum DramBw {
 2662:     /// Latency-only (pre-increment behavior): the ablation mode and the
 2663:     /// event-vs-const equivalence-differential mode — the constant path
 2664:     /// has no bandwidth concept, so equivalence is proven here.
 2665:     Unmodeled { arch: ArchId },
 2666:     /// Configured carded aggregate service rate; runner/default provenance is
 2667:     /// carried by the direct-run configuration rather than inferred here.
 2668:     Carded {
 2669:         arch: ArchId,
 2670:         bytes_per_sec: u64,
 2671:         sm_clock_hz: u64,
 2672:     },
 2673:     /// Opt-in carded service with transactional dirty-sector writeback.
 2674:     /// Capacity is measured in 32-byte dirty sectors; this remains a bounded
 2675:     /// native replay experiment, not a coherence/ownership protocol.
 2676:     CardedWriteback {
 2677:         arch: ArchId,
 2678:         bytes_per_sec: u64,
 2679:         sm_clock_hz: u64,
 2680:         capacity_sectors: u32,
 2681:     },
 2682: }
 2683: 
 2684: impl DramBw {
 2685:     pub const fn arch(self) -> ArchId {
 2686:         match self {
 2687:             Self::Unmodeled { arch }
 2688:             | Self::Carded { arch, .. }
 2689:             | Self::CardedWriteback { arch, .. } => arch,
 2690:         }
 2691:     }
 2692: 
 2693:     pub const fn writeback_capacity_sectors(self) -> Option<u32> {
 2694:         match self {
 2695:             Self::CardedWriteback {
 2696:                 capacity_sectors, ..
 2697:             } => Some(capacity_sectors),
 2698:             _ => None,
 2699:         }
 2700:     }
 2701: 
 2702:     pub const fn service_rate_bytes_per_sec(self) -> Option<u64> {
 2703:         match self {
 2704:             Self::Carded { bytes_per_sec, .. } | Self::CardedWriteback { bytes_per_sec, .. } => {
 2705:                 Some(bytes_per_sec)
 2706:             }
 2707:             Self::Unmodeled { .. } => None,
 2708:         }
 2709:     }
 2710: }
 2711: 
 2712: fn writeback_queue_unavailable(dram: DramBw) -> bool {
 2713:     !matches!(dram, DramBw::CardedWriteback { .. })
 2714: }
 2715: 
 2716: /// The G/D/1 byte server. Fixed-point time in units of
 2717: /// `cycle × bytes_per_sec` (u128): serving `b` bytes takes
 2718: /// `b × sm_clock_hz` fp-units (= b × Hz / Bps cycles), so no division
 2719: /// happens until the final ceil back to cycles — bit-exact and
 2720: /// deterministic in queue order.
 2721: struct DramServer {
 2722:     free_fp: u128,
 2723:     bytes_per_sec: u128,
 2724:     sm_clock_hz: u128,
 2725: }
 2726: 
 2727: impl DramServer {
 2728:     #[cfg(feature = "reference")]
 2729:     fn typed_boundary_state(&self) -> Result<DramBoundaryState, DramStateError> {
 2730:         DramBoundaryState::from_fixed_point(
 2731:             u64::try_from(self.bytes_per_sec).map_err(|_| DramStateError::InvalidRate)?,
 2732:             u64::try_from(self.sm_clock_hz).map_err(|_| DramStateError::InvalidRate)?,
 2733:             self.free_fp,
 2734:         )
 2735:     }
 2736: 
 2737:     /// The caller (`simulate_kernel_shared`) rejects zero rates with a
 2738:     /// typed error before construction — no clamping here (a clamp
 2739:     /// would turn a bad card into a silently absurd 1 B/s server and
 2740:     /// arm the fixed-point u64 ceiling on large traces; gate finding
 2741:     /// 2026-07-07).
 2742:     fn new(bw: DramBw) -> Option<Self> {
 2743:         match bw {
 2744:             DramBw::Unmodeled { .. } => None,
 2745:             DramBw::Carded {
 2746:                 bytes_per_sec,
 2747:                 sm_clock_hz,
 2748:                 ..
 2749:             } => Some(Self {
 2750:                 free_fp: 0,
 2751:                 bytes_per_sec: u128::from(bytes_per_sec),
 2752:                 sm_clock_hz: u128::from(sm_clock_hz),
 2753:             }),
 2754:             // The opt-in writeback path owns the shared byte server through
 2755:             // `WritebackQueue`; no independent read server is created here.
 2756:             DramBw::CardedWriteback { .. } => None,
 2757:         }
 2758:     }
 2759: 
 2760:     /// Compute a service completion without mutating the server. The
 2761:     /// caller uses this to preflight the request's exact outcome-dependent
 2762:     /// fetch before any L2 or accounting state commits.
 2763:     fn preview(&self, arrival: u64, bytes: u64) -> Result<(u128, u64), SystemError> {
 2764:         let arrival_fp = u128::from(arrival).checked_mul(self.bytes_per_sec).ok_or(
 2765:             SystemError::DramFixedPointOverflow {
 2766:                 operation: "arrival scaling",
 2767:             },
 2768:         )?;
 2769:         let service_fp = u128::from(bytes).checked_mul(self.sm_clock_hz).ok_or(
 2770:             SystemError::DramFixedPointOverflow {
 2771:                 operation: "service scaling",
 2772:             },
 2773:         )?;
 2774:         let start = arrival_fp.max(self.free_fp);
 2775:         let done = start
 2776:             .checked_add(service_fp)
 2777:             .ok_or(SystemError::DramFixedPointOverflow {
 2778:                 operation: "service completion",
 2779:             })?;
 2780:         let ready_fp = done.div_ceil(self.bytes_per_sec);
 2781:         let ready = u64::try_from(ready_fp).map_err(|_| SystemError::DramCycleOverflow {
 2782:             completion_fp: done,
 2783:             bytes_per_sec: self.bytes_per_sec,
 2784:         })?;
 2785:         Ok((done, ready))
 2786:     }
 2787: }
 2788: 
 2789: /// One address-space origin's DRAM observer lane.
 2790: #[derive(Debug, Default, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
 2791: pub struct DramOriginStats {
 2792:     pub read_sectors: u64,
 2793:     pub bw_delayed_responses: u64,
 2794: }
 2795: 
 2796: /// Driver-side DRAM traffic and timing-neutral lower-level topology observer.
 2797: #[derive(Debug, Default, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
 2798: pub struct DramStats {
 2799:     /// Architecture provenance bound by the shared-L2 system. Unbound
 2800:     /// synthetic stats are rejected before aggregation or emission.
 2801:     arch: Option<ArchId>,
 2802:     pub service_rate_bytes_per_sec: Option<u64>,
 2803:     /// 32 B DRAM read fetches DEMANDED by the run (L2-missing sectors
 2804:     /// of read operations plus missing sectors of ATOM and RED internal RMW reads).
 2805:     /// Counted in BOTH modes: under `Unmodeled` it audits the ablation's demand
 2806:     /// with no server attached. Plain stores never enter this modeled
 2807:     /// read-fetch count.
 2808:     pub read_sectors: u64,
 2809:     /// Missing-sector read demand attributable to returning ATOM operations.
 2810:     /// This is a traffic observer, not a claim that the value-dependent RMW
 2811:     /// serialization is complete.
 2812:     pub atomic_rmw_read_sectors: u64,
 2813:     /// Missing-sector read demand attributable to non-returning RED/REDG
 2814:     /// operations. The reduction result/coherence protocol remains a typed
 2815:     /// tail even though its cold-line read arm is charged here.
 2816:     pub reduction_rmw_read_sectors: u64,
 2817:     /// Responses whose ready time the bandwidth server pushed PAST the
 2818:     /// latency arm (0 in uncongested runs and in `Unmodeled` mode).
 2819:     /// Late sector follow-ups carry their own completion response and are
 2820:     /// therefore included in the same latency-arm accounting as any other
 2821:     /// reply-bearing read.
 2822:     pub bw_delayed_responses: u64,
 2823:     /// L2 replacement topology. In the default read-only path dirty entries
 2824:     /// are candidate writeback work; the opt-in writeback variant also reports
 2825:     /// separate completion counters below.
 2826:     pub l2_evicted_lines: u64,
 2827:     pub l2_evicted_valid_sectors: u64,
 2828:     pub l2_evicted_pending_lines: u64,
 2829:     pub l2_evicted_pending_sectors: u64,
 2830:     pub l2_dirty_eviction_lines: u64,
 2831:     pub l2_dirty_evicted_sectors: u64,
 2832:     /// Opt-in scheduled writeback completion observations, including eviction
 2833:     /// victims and the terminal resident-L2 flush in CardedWriteback mode.
 2834:     pub writeback_completed_lines: u64,
 2835:     pub writeback_completed_sectors: u64,
 2836:     pub writeback_completed_bytes: u64,
 2837:     /// Subset of the writeback completion stream sourced from resident L1
 2838:     /// dirty sectors; L2 eviction/resident flush counters remain separate.
 2839:     pub l1_writeback_completed_lines: u64,
 2840:     pub l1_writeback_completed_sectors: u64,
 2841:     pub l1_writeback_completed_bytes: u64,
 2842:     /// Logical payload-forwarding records validated at terminal L1 flush.
 2843:     /// These counters prove model-local byte conservation only; they are not
 2844:     /// physical DRAM acknowledgements.
 2845:     pub l1_payload_forwarded_sectors: u64,
 2846:     pub l1_payload_forwarded_bytes: u64,
 2847:     pub l1_payload_forwarding_digest_xor: u64,
 2848:     pub writeback_completion_frontier: Option<u64>,
 2849:     /// Opt-in directory-service completion observations, separate from
 2850:     /// writeback and read-traffic counters.
 2851:     pub coherence_completed_transactions: u64,
 2852:     pub coherence_completed_sectors: u64,
 2853:     pub coherence_completion_frontier: Option<u64>,
 2854:     /// Maximum terminal dirty residency across the launches aggregated into
 2855:     /// this observer. A gauge is never summed as traffic.
 2856:     pub maximum_terminal_l2_dirty_lines: u64,
 2857:     pub maximum_terminal_l2_dirty_sectors: u64,
 2858:     pub maximum_terminal_l1_dirty_lines: u64,
 2859:     pub maximum_terminal_l1_dirty_sectors: u64,
 2860:     /// Observer-only effective partition/slice pressure candidates. The
 2861:     /// mapping uses line-id modulo/XOR hypotheses; it is not physical GA102
 2862:     /// swizzling and never feeds the byte server.
 2863:     pub dram_partition_modulo_sectors: [u64; 12],
 2864:     pub dram_partition_xor_sectors: [u64; 12],
 2865:     pub dram_slice_modulo_sectors: [u64; 6],
 2866:     by_origin: [DramOriginStats; 2],
 2867: }
 2868: 
 2869: impl DramStats {
 2870:     pub fn for_arch(arch: ArchId) -> Self {
 2871:         Self {
 2872:             arch: Some(arch),
 2873:             ..Self::default()
 2874:         }
 2875:     }
 2876: 
 2877:     pub fn with_service_rate(mut self, rate: Option<u64>) -> Self {
 2878:         self.service_rate_bytes_per_sec = rate;
 2879:         self
 2880:     }
 2881: 
 2882:     pub fn arch(&self) -> Option<ArchId> {
 2883:         self.arch
 2884:     }
 2885: 
 2886:     pub fn origin(&self, origin: MemoryOrigin) -> DramOriginStats {
 2887:         self.by_origin[origin.index()]
 2888:     }
 2889: 
 2890:     fn with_l2_eviction(mut self, eviction: L2Eviction) -> Result<Self, SystemError> {
 2891:         macro_rules! add {
 2892:             ($field:ident, $value:expr, $name:literal) => {
 2893:                 self.$field = self
 2894:                     .$field
 2895:                     .checked_add($value)
 2896:                     .ok_or(SystemError::CounterOverflow { counter: $name })?;
 2897:             };
 2898:         }
 2899:         add!(l2_evicted_lines, 1, "L2 evicted lines");
 2900:         add!(
 2901:             l2_evicted_valid_sectors,
 2902:             u64::from(eviction.valid_mask.count_ones()),
 2903:             "L2 evicted valid sectors"
 2904:         );
 2905:         if eviction.pending_mask != 0 {
 2906:             add!(l2_evicted_pending_lines, 1, "L2 evicted pending lines");
 2907:             add!(
 2908:                 l2_evicted_pending_sectors,
 2909:                 u64::from(eviction.pending_mask.count_ones()),
 2910:                 "L2 evicted pending sectors"
 2911:             );
 2912:         }
 2913:         if eviction.dirty_mask != 0 {
 2914:             add!(l2_dirty_eviction_lines, 1, "L2 dirty eviction lines");
 2915:             add!(
 2916:                 l2_dirty_evicted_sectors,
 2917:                 u64::from(eviction.dirty_mask.count_ones()),
 2918:                 "L2 dirty evicted sectors"
 2919:             );
 2920:         }
 2921:         Ok(self)
 2922:     }
 2923: 
 2924:     fn with_terminal_l2_dirty_residency(
 2925:         mut self,
 2926:         residency: L2DirtyResidency,
 2927:     ) -> Result<Self, SystemError> {
 2928:         let lines = u64::try_from(residency.lines).map_err(|_| SystemError::CounterOverflow {
 2929:             counter: "terminal L2 dirty resident lines",
 2930:         })?;
 2931:         let sectors =
 2932:             u64::try_from(residency.sectors).map_err(|_| SystemError::CounterOverflow {
 2933:                 counter: "terminal L2 dirty resident sectors",
 2934:             })?;
 2935:         self.maximum_terminal_l2_dirty_lines = self.maximum_terminal_l2_dirty_lines.max(lines);
 2936:         self.maximum_terminal_l2_dirty_sectors =
 2937:             self.maximum_terminal_l2_dirty_sectors.max(sectors);
 2938:         Ok(self)
 2939:     }
 2940: 
 2941:     fn with_terminal_l1_dirty_residency(
 2942:         mut self,
 2943:         residency: sim_mem::L1DirtyResidency,
 2944:     ) -> Result<Self, SystemError> {
 2945:         self.maximum_terminal_l1_dirty_lines =
 2946:             self.maximum_terminal_l1_dirty_lines.max(residency.lines);
 2947:         self.maximum_terminal_l1_dirty_sectors = self
 2948:             .maximum_terminal_l1_dirty_sectors
 2949:             .max(residency.sectors);
 2950:         Ok(self)
 2951:     }
 2952: 
 2953:     fn with_partition_candidate(
 2954:         mut self,
 2955:         line: i64,
 2956:         sectors: u64,
 2957:     ) -> Result<Self, SystemError> {
 2958:         let line = u64::try_from(line).unwrap_or_default();
 2959:         let modulo = usize::try_from(line % 12).unwrap_or(0);
 2960:         let xor = usize::try_from((line ^ (line >> 7)) % 12).unwrap_or(0);
 2961:         let slice = usize::try_from(line % 6).unwrap_or(0);
 2962:         self.dram_partition_modulo_sectors[modulo] = self.dram_partition_modulo_sectors[modulo]
 2963:             .checked_add(sectors)
 2964:             .ok_or(SystemError::CounterOverflow {
 2965:                 counter: "DRAM modulo partition candidate sectors",
 2966:             })?;
 2967:         self.dram_partition_xor_sectors[xor] = self.dram_partition_xor_sectors[xor]
 2968:             .checked_add(sectors)
 2969:             .ok_or(SystemError::CounterOverflow {
 2970:                 counter: "DRAM XOR partition candidate sectors",
 2971:             })?;
 2972:         self.dram_slice_modulo_sectors[slice] = self.dram_slice_modulo_sectors[slice]
 2973:             .checked_add(sectors)
 2974:             .ok_or(SystemError::CounterOverflow {
 2975:                 counter: "DRAM slice candidate sectors",
 2976:             })?;
 2977:         Ok(self)
 2978:     }
 2979: 
 2980:     /// Build a coherent synthetic observer from explicit origin lanes.
 2981:     pub fn from_origins(
 2982:         arch: ArchId,
 2983:         global: DramOriginStats,
 2984:         local: DramOriginStats,
 2985:     ) -> Result<Self, SystemCounterError> {
 2986:         let read_sectors = global.read_sectors.checked_add(local.read_sectors).ok_or(
 2987:             SystemCounterError::DramOriginPartitionOverflow {
 2988:                 counter: "read sectors",
 2989:                 global: global.read_sectors,
 2990:                 local: local.read_sectors,
 2991:             },
 2992:         )?;
 2993:         let bw_delayed_responses = global
 2994:             .bw_delayed_responses
 2995:             .checked_add(local.bw_delayed_responses)
 2996:             .ok_or(SystemCounterError::DramOriginPartitionOverflow {
 2997:                 counter: "bandwidth-delayed responses",
 2998:                 global: global.bw_delayed_responses,
 2999:                 local: local.bw_delayed_responses,
 3000:             })?;
 3001:         Ok(Self {
 3002:             arch: Some(arch),
 3003:             service_rate_bytes_per_sec: None,
 3004:             read_sectors,
 3005:             atomic_rmw_read_sectors: 0,
 3006:             reduction_rmw_read_sectors: 0,
 3007:             bw_delayed_responses,
 3008:             l2_evicted_lines: 0,
 3009:             l2_evicted_valid_sectors: 0,
 3010:             l2_evicted_pending_lines: 0,
 3011:             l2_evicted_pending_sectors: 0,
 3012:             l2_dirty_eviction_lines: 0,
 3013:             l2_dirty_evicted_sectors: 0,
 3014:             writeback_completed_lines: 0,
 3015:             writeback_completed_sectors: 0,
 3016:             writeback_completed_bytes: 0,
 3017:             l1_writeback_completed_lines: 0,
 3018:             l1_writeback_completed_sectors: 0,
 3019:             l1_writeback_completed_bytes: 0,
 3020:             l1_payload_forwarded_sectors: 0,
 3021:             l1_payload_forwarded_bytes: 0,
 3022:             l1_payload_forwarding_digest_xor: 0,
 3023:             writeback_completion_frontier: None,
 3024:             coherence_completed_transactions: 0,
 3025:             coherence_completed_sectors: 0,
 3026:             coherence_completion_frontier: None,
 3027:             maximum_terminal_l2_dirty_lines: 0,
 3028:             maximum_terminal_l2_dirty_sectors: 0,
 3029:             maximum_terminal_l1_dirty_lines: 0,
 3030:             maximum_terminal_l1_dirty_sectors: 0,
 3031:             dram_partition_modulo_sectors: [0; 12],
 3032:             dram_partition_xor_sectors: [0; 12],
 3033:             dram_slice_modulo_sectors: [0; 6],
 3034:             by_origin: [global, local],
 3035:         })
 3036:     }
 3037: 
 3038:     /// Explicitly attribute an already-built unified synthetic observer to one
 3039:     /// origin. Modeled requests always mutate an explicit lane directly.
 3040:     pub fn with_unified_origin(mut self, origin: MemoryOrigin) -> Self {
 3041:         let unified = DramOriginStats {
 3042:             read_sectors: self.read_sectors,
 3043:             bw_delayed_responses: self.bw_delayed_responses,
 3044:         };
 3045:         self.by_origin = [DramOriginStats::default(); 2];
 3046:         self.by_origin[origin.index()] = unified;
 3047:         self
 3048:     }
 3049: 
 3050:     pub fn validate_origin_partition(&self) -> Result<(), SystemCounterError> {
 3051:         let global = self.origin(MemoryOrigin::Global);
 3052:         let local = self.origin(MemoryOrigin::Local);
 3053:         let read_sectors = global.read_sectors.checked_add(local.read_sectors).ok_or(
 3054:             SystemCounterError::DramOriginPartitionOverflow {
 3055:                 counter: "read sectors",
 3056:                 global: global.read_sectors,
 3057:                 local: local.read_sectors,
 3058:             },
 3059:         )?;
 3060:         if self.read_sectors != read_sectors {

 3577: #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
 3578: pub(crate) fn simulate_kernel_shared_with_residency_and_candidates_with_coherence_and_payload_events(
 3579:     per_sm_blocks: &BTreeMap<i32, Vec<BlockReplay>>,
 3580:     schedule: &FunctionSchedule,
 3581:     params: &IssueModelParams,
 3582:     shared_memory_residency: SharedMemoryResidency,
 3583:     l1_config: L1Config,
 3584:     l2: &mut L2Model,
 3585:     dram: DramBw,
 3586:     memory_service_candidates: &[MemoryServiceCandidate],
 3587:     memory_admission_candidates: &[MemoryAdmissionCandidate],
 3588:     memory_admission_feedback: Option<MemoryAdmissionCandidate>,
 3589:     coherence_config: Option<CoherenceConfig>,
 3590:     payload_events: &[L1PayloadAdmissionEvent],
 3591: ) -> Result<SystemResult, SystemError> {
 3592:     let arch = schedule.arch;
 3593:     require_arch(arch, params.arch, "issue parameters")?;
 3594:     require_arch(arch, l1_config.arch, "L1 configuration")?;
 3595:     require_arch(arch, l2.config().arch, "L2 configuration")?;
 3596:     checked_l2_counters(arch, l2)?;
 3597:     require_arch(arch, dram.arch(), "DRAM configuration")?;
 3598:     require_l2_pending_read_semantics(arch, l2.config().is_effective())?;
 3599:     let memory_admission_feedback = memory_admission_feedback
 3600:         .filter(|_| qualifies_store_fence_feedback(schedule));
 3601:     params.validate_l1_timing(l1_config)?;
 3602:     // Fail closed on an inverted ladder: every horizon/run-ahead bound
 3603:     // below assumes no response can price under the L2-hit arm.
 3604:     if params.global_flat_latency < params.l1_miss_latency {
 3605:         return Err(SystemError::LatencyLadderInverted {
 3606:             l2_hit: params.l1_miss_latency,
 3607:             dram: params.global_flat_latency,
 3608:         });
 3609:     }
 3610:     // Fail closed on a zero bandwidth rate: a clamped server would be
 3611:     // absurd (1 B/s) or silently inert (0 Hz), and a 1 B/s server arms
 3612:     // the fixed-point u64 ceiling on large traces.
 3613:     let rate = match dram {
 3614:         DramBw::Carded {
 3615:             bytes_per_sec,
 3616:             sm_clock_hz,
 3617:             ..
 3618:         }
 3619:         | DramBw::CardedWriteback {
 3620:             bytes_per_sec,
 3621:             sm_clock_hz,
 3622:             ..
 3623:         } => Some((bytes_per_sec, sm_clock_hz)),
 3624:         DramBw::Unmodeled { .. } => None,
 3625:     };
 3626:     if let Some((bytes_per_sec, sm_clock_hz)) = rate
 3627:         && (bytes_per_sec == 0 || sm_clock_hz == 0)
 3628:     {
 3629:         return Err(SystemError::DramBandwidthInvalid {
 3630:             bytes_per_sec,
 3631:             sm_clock_hz,
 3632:         });
 3633:     }
 3634:     if u32::try_from(per_sm_blocks.len()).is_err() {
 3635:         return Err(SystemError::ActorCountOutOfRange {
 3636:             count: per_sm_blocks.len(),
 3637:         });
 3638:     }
 3639:     validate_memory_service_candidates(memory_service_candidates).map_err(IssueError::from)?;
 3640:     validate_memory_admission_candidates(memory_admission_candidates).map_err(IssueError::from)?;
 3641:     if let Some(candidate) = memory_admission_feedback {
 3642:         candidate.validate_feedback(params.read_barrier_latency)?;
 3643:     }
 3644:     let shared_write_lookup_observer = stateful_write_lookup_observer_enabled(schedule)
 3645:         .then(|| L1WriteLookupObserver::for_arch(arch))
 3646:         .transpose()
 3647:         .map_err(IssueError::from)?
 3648:         .map(|observer| Rc::new(RefCell::new(observer)));
 3649:     let sm_ids: Vec<i32> = per_sm_blocks.keys().copied().collect();
 3650:     let l1_visibility_protocol =
 3651:         coherence_config.is_some_and(CoherenceConfig::l1_visibility_protocol);
 3652:     let l1_resident_writeback =
 3653:         coherence_config.is_some_and(CoherenceConfig::l1_resident_writeback);
 3654:     if l1_resident_writeback && !l1_visibility_protocol {
 3655:         return Err(SystemError::L1DirtyWritebackRequiresVisibilityProtocol);
 3656:     }
 3657:     if l1_resident_writeback
 3658:         && (writeback_queue_unavailable(dram)
 3659:             || !memory_service_candidates.is_empty()
 3660:             || !memory_admission_candidates.is_empty()
 3661:             || memory_admission_feedback.is_some())
 3662:     {
 3663:         return Err(if writeback_queue_unavailable(dram) {
 3664:             SystemError::L1DirtyWritebackRequiresQueue
 3665:         } else {
 3666:             SystemError::L1DirtyWritebackCandidateConflict
 3667:         });
 3668:     }
 3669:     let mut cores: Vec<SmCore<'_>> = per_sm_blocks
 3670:         .values()
 3671:         .enumerate()
 3672:         .map(|(sm_ix, blocks)| {
 3673:             let mut core = SmCore::new_with_residency_and_observer(
 3674:                 blocks,
 3675:                 schedule,
 3676:                 params,
 3677:                 shared_memory_residency,
 3678:                 SmMemMode::L1Shared(l1_config),
 3679:                 shared_write_lookup_observer.clone(),
 3680:             )?;
 3681:             if l1_visibility_protocol {
 3682:                 let requester =
 3683:                     u8::try_from(sm_ids[sm_ix]).map_err(|_| IssueError::CounterOverflow {
 3684:                         counter: "L1 visibility requester domain",
 3685:                     })?;
 3686:                 core.enable_l1_visibility_observations(requester)?;
 3687:             }
 3688:             if l1_resident_writeback {
 3689:                 core.enable_l1_resident_writeback()?;
 3690:             }
 3691:             core.install_memory_service_candidates(memory_service_candidates)?;
 3692:             if !memory_admission_candidates.is_empty() {
 3693:                 core.enable_memory_admission_observations();
 3694:             }
 3695:             if let Some(candidate) = memory_admission_feedback {
 3696:                 core.install_memory_admission_feedback(candidate)?;
 3697:             }
 3698:             Ok::<_, IssueError>(core)
 3699:         })
 3700:         .collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;
 3701:     let observe_admission = !memory_admission_candidates.is_empty();
 3702:     let feedback_admission = memory_admission_feedback.is_some();
 3703:     let observe_admission_groups = observe_admission || feedback_admission;
 3704:     let mut admission_states = observe_admission
 3705:         .then(|| {
 3706:             (0..cores.len())
 3707:                 .map(|_| MemoryAdmissionCandidateState::checked(memory_admission_candidates))
 3708:                 .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()
 3709:         })
 3710:         .transpose()
 3711:         .map_err(IssueError::from)?;
 3712:     let mut admission_observers = observe_admission_groups.then(|| {
 3713:         (0..cores.len())
 3714:             .map(|_| AdmissionInstructionObserver::default())
 3715:             .collect::<Vec<_>>()
 3716:     });
 3717: 
 3718:     let mut queue: EventQueue<Ev> = EventQueue::new();
 3719:     #[cfg(feature = "performance-counters")]
 3720:     let batch_replyless_writes = memory_admission_feedback.is_none()
 3721:         && std::env::var_os("SIM_RUN_DISABLE_L2_ACCESS_BATCHING").is_none();
 3722:     #[cfg(not(feature = "performance-counters"))]
 3723:     let batch_replyless_writes = memory_admission_feedback.is_none();
 3724:     let mut scratch: Vec<MemRequest> = Vec::new();
 3725:     let mut l1_visibility_observations = Vec::new();
 3726:     let mut l1_fence_observations = Vec::new();
 3727:     let mut l1_fill_visibility_observations = Vec::new();
 3728:     // Scheduled-but-unapplied response times PER SM: the only external
 3729:     // events that can affect an SM's own state, hence its advance
 3730:     // horizon. Other SMs' events are deliberately NOT barriers — cross-SM
 3731:     // effects flow only through the L2 access stream, which the queue
 3732:     // already orders by (time, seq). (Treating every queued event as a
 3733:     // barrier would also livelock: two SMs parked at the same timestamp
 3734:     // would bounce off each other's wake events forever.)
 3735:     let mut response_due = DueTimes::new(cores.len());
 3736:     // Opt-in STORE/RED completions are self-affecting external events: a
 3737:     // matching MEMBAR remains unpriced until the event is processed, and the
 3738:     // actor must not run past the earliest pending acknowledgement.
 3739:     let mut write_ack_due = DueTimes::new(cores.len());
 3740:     // Pending wake times PER SM, deduplicating at the source: a wake is
 3741:     // scheduled only when no pending wake at an earlier-or-equal time
 3742:     // covers it (an earlier wake re-parks and reschedules if still
 3743:     // needed). Without this, every response that wakes an SM which then
 3744:     // re-parks at the same future horizon piles up another stale wake —
 3745:     // hundreds popping back-to-back at one timestamp on real cells —
 3746:     // wasted heap traffic AND an unbounded idle-wake streak the livelock
 3747:     // guard could not be told apart from a real spin.
 3748:     let mut wake_due = DueTimes::new(cores.len());
 3749:     // Producer-supplied payload admissions are self-affecting cache events;
 3750:     // keep their due frontier separate from responses, write acknowledgements,
 3751:     // and ordinary wakes so an actor cannot run past the mutation.
 3752:     let mut payload_due = DueTimes::new(cores.len());
 3753:     let mut unpriced = UnpricedBounds((0..cores.len()).map(|_| BTreeMap::new()).collect());
 3754:     let mut pending_l2_ready = PendingL2Ready::default();
 3755:     let mut l2_by_source = L2SourceSplit::for_arch(arch);
 3756:     let mut dram_server = DramServer::new(dram);
 3757:     let mut writeback_queue = match dram {
 3758:         DramBw::CardedWriteback {
 3759:             capacity_sectors,
 3760:             bytes_per_sec,
 3761:             sm_clock_hz,
 3762:             ..
 3763:         } => Some(WritebackQueue::new(
 3764:             capacity_sectors,
 3765:             bytes_per_sec,
 3766:             sm_clock_hz,
 3767:         )?),
 3768:         _ => None,
 3769:     };
 3770:     let mut coherence_queue = coherence_config.map(CoherenceQueue::new);
 3771:     let mut l1_visibility_queue = if l1_visibility_protocol {
 3772:         let config = coherence_config.ok_or(SystemError::L1VisibilityProtocolRequiresCoherence)?;
 3773:         Some(L1VisibilityQueue::new(L1VisibilityConfig::new(
 3774:             config.service_cycles_per_sector(),
 3775:             config.capacity_sectors(),
 3776:         )?))
 3777:     } else {
 3778:         None
 3779:     };
 3780:     let mut dram_stats =
 3781:         DramStats::for_arch(arch).with_service_rate(dram.service_rate_bytes_per_sec());
 3782:     // Seed actor wakes before producer events.  At equal cycle zero this lets
 3783:     // the actor observe `payload_due` and park before the value mutation is
 3784:     // applied, rather than allowing a queued payload admission to run ahead
 3785:     // of the first modeled issue boundary merely because it was inserted
 3786:     // first.  The separate payload horizon still prevents run-ahead at every
 3787:     // later timestamp.
 3788:     for ix in 0..cores.len() {
 3789:         let target = u32::try_from(ix)
 3790:             .map_err(|_| SystemError::ActorCountOutOfRange { count: cores.len() })?;

  390:     /// Trace-topology lookup hits for write-class sectors. This observer uses
  391:     /// 64 B lookup allocation within each ideal L1TEX wavefront and does not
  392:     /// mutate cache tags, fills, replacement, or timing.
  393:     pub write_lookup_hits: u64,
  394:     /// RED/REDG sectors and sector-valid hits, kept separate from ordinary STG.
  395:     pub reduction_sectors: u64,
  396:     pub reduction_hits: u64,

  398: 
  399: impl L1OriginCounters {
  400:     /// Effective L1TEX tag-lookup-hit projection for sector metrics.
  401:     ///
  402:     /// The timing cache keeps all pending sectors as `merges` because they
  403:     /// share one in-flight fill and must not become independent timing hits.
  404:     /// The profiler lookup surface is a tag observation: a fresh line
  405:     /// allocation contributes one tag miss, while the remaining sectors that
  406:     /// merge onto that allocation are lookup hits.  Therefore the bounded
  407:     /// observer starts with resident hits plus all merge observations and
  408:     /// removes one miss for each fresh line allocation.  This deliberately
  409:     /// does not alter tags, fills, MSHRs, replacement, or replay timing.
  410:     pub const fn effective_lookup_hits(&self) -> u64 {
  411:         let projected = self
  412:             .hits
  413:             .saturating_add(self.merges)
  414:             .saturating_sub(self.line_alloc_misses);
  415:         // The observer is a projection over the modeled sector domain.  A
  416:         // partially decoded/coalesced trace can expose more merge labels than
  417:         // the retained sector denominator; keep the projection typed and
  418:         // bounded rather than aborting an otherwise valid real launch.
  419:         let denominator = self.lookup_sectors();
  420:         if projected < denominator {
  421:             projected
  422:         } else {
  423:             denominator
  424:         }
  425:     }
  426: 
  427:     /// Total read-sector lookups represented by this origin lane.
  428:     pub const fn lookup_sectors(&self) -> u64 {
  429:         self.hits.saturating_add(self.misses)
  430:     }
  431: }
  432: 
  433: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
  434: pub struct L1Counters {
  435:     /// Architecture provenance bound by the owning [`L1Cache`]. `None` is
  436:     /// reserved for synthetic/unbound snapshots and is rejected at emission
  437:     /// or aggregation boundaries.
  438:     arch: Option<ArchId>,
  439:     /// Ported counters (modeled, manifest-eligible accesses only).
  440:     pub hits: u64,
  441:     pub misses: u64,
  442:     pub merges: u64,
  443:     pub pending_lookup_hits: u64,
  444:     pub stall_events: u64,
  445:     /// Number of miss attempts observed while the finite L1 MSHR set was
  446:     /// full. These pressure fields are unified cache observers and are not
  447:     /// partitioned into global/local traffic lanes.
  448:     pub mshr_stall_occupancy_samples: u64,
  449:     /// Sum of MSHR entries in use at those full-set stall observations.
  450:     pub mshr_stall_occupancy_sum: u64,
  451:     pub line_fills: u64,
  452:     pub line_alloc_misses: u64,
  453:     pub sector_resident_misses: u64,
  454:     /// Manifest-tagged ineligible sectors (the ported bypass counter).
  455:     pub bypass_sectors: u64,
  456:     /// Subset of `bypass_sectors` whose cache-op token is ATOM (the
  457:     /// write-class bypass stream). Kept as a subset counter so the
  458:     /// emission layer can fold the READ-class bypass stream (LDGSTS)
  459:     /// into the global-load sector surface without pulling atomics in
  460:     /// with it — NCU books those under op_atom/op_red, not op_ld.
  461:     pub bypass_atom_sectors: u64,
  462:     /// v0 extensions for the unified-denominator emission.
  463:     pub untagged_sectors: u64,
  464:     pub store_sectors: u64,
  465:     pub store_hits: u64,
  466:     /// §6.4 write-class line-tag observer used by the SM86 compatibility
  467:     /// surface. It is a superset of sector-valid `store_hits`; SM120's
  468:     /// unified sector-hit projection uses the effective model's resident
  469:     /// sector counters instead because no SM120 write-lookup capability is
  470:     /// established.
  471:     pub store_tag_hits: u64,
  472:     pub write_lookup_hits: u64,
  473:     /// RED/REDG sectors and sector-valid hits. They contribute to the unified
  474:     /// L1TEX observer but never to the plain global-store surface.
  475:     pub reduction_sectors: u64,
  476:     pub reduction_hits: u64,
  477:     /// Accounting provenance only. Cache tags, fills, MSHRs, recency, and
  478:     /// replacement remain unified modeled cache state.
  479:     by_origin: [L1OriginCounters; 2],
  480: }

 1229: /// Observer-only tag window for Ampere L1TEX write lookups.  It is separate
 1230: /// from the modeled read-state cache: the latter remains the validated
 1231: /// fully-associative replay approximation, while this state models the
 1232: /// write-allocate request stream used by the NCU `op_st_lookup_*` counters.

 1234: pub struct L1WriteLookupObserver {
 1235:     config: L1WriteLookupWindow,
 1236:     /// Each set is kept in LRU-to-MRU order.  A store instruction evaluates
 1237:     /// all sectors of a line against the pre-request tag, then touches it once
 1238:     /// (same-request sector coalescing therefore remains a miss).
 1239:     sets: Vec<Vec<i64>>,
 1240: }
 1241: 
 1242: /// Fallible-free state transition prepared for one write-observer batch.  The
 1243: /// plan clones only sets touched by the batch (rather than the complete
 1244: /// observer), so callers can defer publication across a larger instruction
 1245: /// transaction without turning every store into an O(total-set-count) clone.
 1246: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
 1247: pub struct L1WriteLookupObserverPlan {
 1248:     hits: u64,
 1249:     sets: Vec<(usize, Vec<i64>)>,
 1250: }

 1254:         self.hits

 1018: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
 1019: pub struct L2Counters {
 1020:     /// Architecture provenance bound by the owning [`L2Model`].
 1021:     arch: Option<ArchId>,
 1022:     pub hits: u64,
 1023:     pub misses: u64,
 1024:     /// NCU-style L1TEX-origin lookup hits. In addition to completed-data hits,
 1025:     /// this counts a read merging into a pending allocation and every
 1026:     /// write-class sector lookup. Cache state and response pricing continue to
 1027:     /// use `hits`/`misses`; this is an observer-only classification.
 1028:     pub lookup_hits: u64,
 1029:     /// NCU-style lookup denominator.  It is deliberately separate from the
 1030:     /// modeled cache-state `hits + misses` stream because L2 write packetization
 1031:     /// can collapse several modeled sector-state mutations into one lookup observer
 1032:     /// unit.  Legacy/direct cache accesses populate it one sector at a time;
 1033:     /// the shared-system replay replaces the observer totals with its
 1034:     /// request/coalescing accounting after the event stream drains.
 1035:     pub lookup_requests: u64,
 1036:     /// Miss split (state-machine kinds, not the Python 3C diagnostic).
 1037:     pub line_misses: u64,
 1038:     pub sector_fill_misses: u64,
 1039:     /// Of `hits`: write-class hits granted on line-TAG residency while
 1040:     /// the sector's valid bit was still clear (the §6.4 rule's whole
 1041:     /// contribution — zero when every access is `Read`-class).
 1042:     pub write_tag_hits: u64,
 1043:     /// Read sectors that joined an already-pending fill owned by the same
 1044:     /// event-mode allocation. This is an observer of the bounded
 1045:     /// coalescing policy; it does not replace cache-state miss accounting.
 1046:     pub pending_merge_sectors: u64,
 1047:     /// Read sectors that acquired a new event-mode fill capability. Together
 1048:     /// with `pending_merge_sectors` this exposes the sectored fill-ownership
 1049:     /// split used by the shared-L2 replay.
 1050:     pub new_fill_sectors: u64,
 1051:     /// Trace-order request counts by the active effective slice hash. The
 1052:     /// fixed array is an observer surface; it does not assert a proprietary
 1053:     /// GA102 crossbar hash or add queue timing.
 1054:     pub slice_accesses: [u64; 16],
 1055: }

 1285:     pub fn total(&self) -> Result<u64, L2CounterError> {
 1286:         self.hits
 1287:             .checked_add(self.misses)
 1288:             .ok_or(L2CounterError::TotalOverflow {
 1289:                 hits: self.hits,
 1290:                 misses: self.misses,
 1291:             })
 1292:     }
 1293: 
 1294:     /// Validate the observer-only lookup pair.  The modeled cache-state stream
 1295:     /// is intentionally not required to equal this pair: packetization and
 1296:     /// source-side coalescing can make their denominators different.
 1297:     pub fn validate_lookup_observer(&self) -> Result<(), L2CounterError> {
 1298:         if self.lookup_hits > self.lookup_requests {
 1299:             return Err(L2CounterError::LookupHitsExceedRequests {
 1300:                 hits: self.lookup_hits,
 1301:                 requests: self.lookup_requests,
 1302:             });
 1303:         }
 1304:         Ok(())
 1305:     }
 1306: }

===== BEGIN H63 FOLLOW-UP STATE (generated after the main bundle) =====
The corpus runner/source consistency fixes are now landed in the working tree:

* `--no-memory-admission-feedback` is an explicit ablation. The H19 candidate is
  auto-selected only for SM86 full/both with `--cache-scope launch`; stream-L2
  suppresses it and records `memory_admission_feedback_scope_suppressed=true`.
  An explicit candidate with stream-L2 is rejected with a clear CLI error.
* A real `simbench_l1_vendor_sgemm_cublas` stream-L2 full replay is `ok` in
  `../sm86_replay_results/h63_stream_l2_scope_20261016_v1/full.jsonl.zst`.
  The row has `cache_scope=stream-l2`, no feedback candidate, and
  `counters.dram.service_rate_bytes_per_sec=825000000000`.
* `DramCounterReport.service_rate_bytes_per_sec` preserves the configured rate
  per launch. The 825 GB/s value remains an absolute unlocked-protocol
  effective-rate hypothesis mapped to the locked card clock, not a clock
  crosswalk or hardware controller claim.
* The corrected post-replay report is
  `../sm86_replay_results/metric_report_h63_scope_label_audit_20261016_v1/`;
  `summary.json` now contains `comparison_view=scope_compatible_secondary`,
  `raw_all_row_ape_authoritative=true`, `raw_all_row_ape_included=false`, and
  `scope_mismatch_policy=retain_rows_exclude_from_raw_all_row_comparison_ape`.
  The old H42/H48 report directories are historical evidence with pre-H63
  headings; do not interpret their `Full compared metrics` heading as raw APE.
* Observer wording has been tightened: modeled cache-state and decoded trace
  line-domain keys are not physical silicon cache/page identities. GPGPU-Sim/
  Accel-Sim material is source inspection only; no binary/runtime/constants are
  imported.

Please incorporate these updates when proposing the next experiment/model.
===== END H63 FOLLOW-UP STATE =====

===== BEGIN H63 V2 REAL-REPLAY UPDATE =====
After the main bundle, release builds/checks were rerun and a fresh real
stream-L2 full replay completed `ok`:
`../sm86_replay_results/h63_stream_l2_scope_20261016_v2/full.jsonl.zst`.
It used exactly 12 workers, `memory_admission_feedback_scope_suppressed=true`,
`ncu_used_as_input=false`, and per-launch
`service_rate_bytes_per_sec=825000000000`. This v2 artifact supersedes the v1
stream-L2 example in the earlier bundle text.
===== END H63 V2 REAL-REPLAY UPDATE =====

===== BEGIN H64 L2 QUEUE NEGATIVE UPDATE =====
A fresh independent RTX 3090 Ti l2_queue_frontier sweep is complete: 288 points,
all correctness-valid. Twenty-repeat medians at W8/load64 unique are 20,765,
28,137,32,334 clock64 cycles for stride 1/8/32. Selected NCU points show only
~4% LTS active/XBAR change and zero selected L1TEX request-stall counters.
This is a negative for a queue capacity/hash/stride-delay timing arm; issue,
occupancy, overlap, and return effects are confounded. Keep H30-H37 observers
only. The next probe must isolate DRAM read demand versus dirty writeback and
coherence completion before any service timing change. Full details:
`docs/sm86_l2_queue_frontier_negative_20261016.md` and the notes outputs under
`workloads/research_probes/l2_queue_frontier/results/h64_*`.
===== END H64 L2 QUEUE NEGATIVE UPDATE =====

===== BEGIN H65 P1 NEGATIVE UPDATE =====
A same-binary SM86 L1TEX probe confirmed cold line loads report zero lookup
hits (21,504 sectors/misses) while output wavefronts are a separate endpoint.
A full 356-root experiment using `hits + pending_lookup_hits` reduced zero-hit
false positives 22/29 to 1/29 but worsened lookup-hit APE 30.71%→33.43% and
lookup-miss APE 58.44%→110.05%. The source was restored; do not promote an
unqualified wavefront formula. Any future correction must be packet/client/form
gated and sector-conserving. Report:
`docs/sm86_l1_wavefront_lookup_negative_20261016.md`.
===== END H65 P1 NEGATIVE UPDATE =====

===== BEGIN H65 LTC128B UPDATE =====
A supported frontend path now exists: CUDA13.2 `ld.global.ca.L2::128B.u32`
compiles to `LDG.E.LTC128B.STRONG.SM`. Matched RTX3090Ti NCU control:
plain `.ca` = 43,008 L2 TEX read misses, 0 promoted, 43,176 DRAM reads;
LTC128B = 172,032 L2 TEX read misses, 129,024 promoted, 172,672 DRAM reads;
L1/global sectors and zero L1 hits are equal. This confirms promotion but is
not yet a simulator timing arm: full form/working-set matrix, NVBit holdout,
and source-scope accounting remain required. Report:
`docs/sm86_ltc128b_ptx_probe_slice_20261016.md`.
===== END H65 LTC128B UPDATE =====

===== BEGIN H66 GCC/L1.5 UPDATE =====
A same-host NCU GCC probe found arithmetic control: 1,764 GCC requests, 952 GCC
L2 sectors, 125 misses, 3,100 DRAM reads; constant arm: 562,919 requests,
1,960 GCC sectors, 269 misses, 276 DRAM reads. The simulator now emits only
observer rows `dram_gcc_instruction_sector_candidate`,
`dram_gcc_constant_sector_candidate`, and
`dram_read_demand_multiclient_candidate_low`; no L2/DRAM/timing state changes.
Full replay passed 356 roots/12,392 launches, report:
`docs/sm86_gcc_demand_observer_slice_20261016.md`. A same-binary NVBit GCC/TEX
crosswalk is required before a multi-client timing model.
===== END H66 GCC/L1.5 UPDATE =====

===== BEGIN H67 L2 WRITE LOOKUP NEGATIVE UPDATE =====
A cold-store same-binary probe reported zero lookup-hit sectors, but a full
356-root present-sector A/B worsened secondary L2 lookup-hit APE 11.77%→33.67%
and lookup-miss 0%→53.16% (source sectors stayed 6.76%). The source was
reverted. Probe and scored source-unit scopes are not like-for-like; require
operation-split NCU/TEX plus NVBit crosswalk before write lookup/coalescing
changes. Report: `docs/sm86_l2_write_lookup_negative_20261016.md`.
===== END H67 L2 WRITE LOOKUP NEGATIVE UPDATE =====

===== BEGIN H68 STORE EXIT-DRAIN UPDATE =====
A five-root real full A/B broadening H19 feedback qualification to all
STORE/RED kernels changed no frontiers or admission exposure; it cannot model
implicit CTA-EXIT drain and was reverted. Existing store_ack matrix tails remain
large, so the next P5 seam must gate EXIT retirement separately using matched
no-fence/fence probe evidence. Report:
`docs/sm86_store_feedback_no_fence_negative_20261016.md`.
===== END H68 STORE EXIT-DRAIN UPDATE =====

===== BEGIN H69 DRAM DEMAND/COMPLETION NEGATIVE UPDATE =====
Audited retained same-binary RTX3090Ti evidence: memory-retirement 102 cells
(94 crosswalk-valid), store-ack 30 cells, launch-cycle controls, and mixed DRAM
streams. No hardware completion token/global order exists; store-tail deltas are
shape-dependent and one DRAM stream falsifies the simulator lower bound while
another is consistent. No timing-active DRAM/writeback/coherence/EXIT model is
promoted. Report: `docs/sm86_dram_demand_completion_negative_20261016.md`.
===== END H69 DRAM DEMAND/COMPLETION NEGATIVE UPDATE =====

===== BEGIN H70 L2 FRONTIER OBSERVER UPDATE =====
The shared-L2 event path now emits timing-neutral per-request issue-to-fill,
issue-to-response, and service-delay sums/maxima plus sample counts. Full-first
real-corpus replay passed 356 roots/12,392 launches with 12 workers and
compressed output at
`../sm86_replay_results/full_corpus_h70_l2_frontier_observer_20261016_v1/`.
No NCU aliases or timing changes. Report:
`docs/sm86_l2_frontier_observer_slice_20261016.md`.
===== END H70 L2 FRONTIER OBSERVER UPDATE =====

--- H71 addendum (2026-10-16) ---

H71 terminal-EXIT store-drain slice is now complete. Independent locked RTX3090Ti
same-binary probe (84 blocks/depth16/active32) measured no-fence
sm__cycles_active.max=10345, smsp max=10327, drain stalls=253415, MEMBAR stalls=0;
explicit MEMBAR.GL measured 11730/11712, drain=1915, MEMBAR=488950. A bounded
store-only candidate flag `exit_waits_for_service_completion` gates only terminal
dynamic EXIT on typed per-warp write acknowledgements; ACK timing is clamped to
its L2 pricing event and stale actor wakes are rearmed at queue_now.

Full-first 12-worker compressed artifacts:
- experimental arm: ../sm86_replay_results/full_corpus_h71_exit_drain_20261016_v2/
- default card (flag false): ../sm86_replay_results/full_corpus_h71_exit_drain_20261016_v3/
Both are 356 roots, 286 ok, 62 control_error, 8 replay_error, 12,392 full
launches. Enabled arm worsens secondary mean APE: SM-active.avg 18.0202->18.1389%,
SMsp-active.avg 17.5137->17.6580%, IPC projection 14.4865->14.5529%, while GPC
improves 12.9008->12.8836%. Default v3 is bit-for-bit H70 baseline. The arm is
therefore disabled in the default card and retained only as an experimental
implementation seam. Report: docs/sm86_exit_drain_slice_20261016.md.

Consultation question: what same-binary NVBit event/metric crosswalk and held-out
probe can map terminal EXIT/drain stalls to the exact STORE acknowledgement
population, separate STORE from RED, and yield an endpoint-matched effective
model without fitting NCU values or perturbing aggregate DRAM/typed tails?

--- H72 addendum (2026-10-16) ---

H72 implemented an observer-only static-SASS scope crosswalk. TraceMetrics now
partitions `global_load_ldg`, `global_load_constant`, `global_load_ltc128b`,
`global_load_strong_gpu`, and `ldgsts_async_copy`; SM86 native metrics expose
plain-LDG and LDGSTS opcode candidates. A post-replay reporter warning for a
gross LTC128B mismatch excludes only L1 global sector/lookup metrics from the
secondary view; no hierarchy traffic or timing is relocated.

Independent controls: cp_async has 32 LDGSTS records/128 L1 sectors with NCU
LDGSTS opcode=32 and global-LD opcode=0 (LDGSTS is included in L1TEX global-op
sectors). SAM H74 has 1024 LDG.E.LTC128B.CONSTANT records but NCU global-LD
requests/sectors=16/40 and TEX read sectors=40, with separate GCC demand.
Vendor/snapshot controls show active LTC128B can still be TEX traffic, so no
universal subtraction or DRAM promotion is valid.

Full-first artifact:
../sm86_replay_results/full_corpus_h72_ldgsts_scope_20261016_v1/
(356 roots, 286/62/8, 12,392 launches, 12 workers, compressed). Report:

--- H121-H122 store-drain timing addendum ---

A new opt-in `exit_waits_for_admission_drain` candidate separated SMSP/L1TEX
admission/XBAR drain from L2 visibility acknowledgements. It schedules typed
acks for event-loop drainage but lets terminal EXIT wait only on the drain
horizon. H121 complete replay: elapsed IPC/GPC 12.2045%/12.8746%, but
SM-active/SMSP regress to 18.2178%/17.7526%. H122 gates drain charging only
when the five-entry SMSP queue is full: elapsed IPC/GPC 12.2864%/12.8892%,
SM-active/SMSP 18.1135%/17.6298%. Both fail the no-regression rule and remain
opt-in. Default H116 scores are unchanged.
